construction Reversing fold of a pair-partition CPM CSS code of arXiv:2609.30069 with J = 3, L = 8, P = 124. Parent qubit q = c*P + t. Parent X row (j,s) meets c*P + ((s - E[j][c]) mod P) for each block c. The involution is pi(c*P + t) = sigma[c]*P + ((-t - B[sigma[c]]) mod P). Folded site i pairs the i-th smallest q with q < pi(q) with pi(q). Each parent X row becomes one generator, X where it meets a representative and Z where it meets a partner.
